📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期埃博拉病毒类群研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 综述识别出多个有前景的布尼迪布焦病毒抗病毒药物候选药物,并总结了以往埃博拉病毒疫情应对中的关键经验教训,为制定更主动的应对策略提供指导。 [10.1038/s41541-026-01569-8]。
  • 各省估计值与全国趋势显著分化,其中伊图里省分层中位数Rt为0.91(95%可信区间[CrI] 0.68-1.24),北基伍省为1.23(0.87-1.72),上韦莱省为1.79(1.24-2.67)。卫生区分析显示出进一步的异质性,部分地区呈下降趋势,而其他地区仍高于阈值。
  • 在接受单克隆抗体鸡尾酒治疗的60名参与者中,95%在6个月随访时对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒血清学检测呈阴性,而对照组中该比例为70%。在接受治疗治疗的参与者中未报告有症状感染病例。 [10.1038/s41591-026-04664-4]。
  • 识别出了不同的蛋白特异性时间替换模式:GP表现出集中在GP1功能区域的多样化替换,L蛋白包含最多的替换坐标,广泛分布在该蛋白中,代表多种时间替换模式;NP表现出混合模式,包括固定和表观逆转;VP35表现出相对保守的氨基酸模式,仅有三个替换位点;VP30和VP24在三个暴发时期内表现出最小的变化;VP40的变化主要以同暴发内多态性为特征,而非持续的时间替换。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The review identifies promising BDBV vaccine candidates and summarizes critical lessons from previous Ebolavirus outbreaks to guide more proactive vaccine strategies. [10.1038/s41541-026-01569-8].
  • Provincial estimates showed significant divergence from the national trend, with Ituri having a hierarchical median Rt of 0.91 (95% credible interval [CrI] 0.68-1.24), Nord-Kivu at 1.23 (0.87-1.72), and Haut-Uele at 1.79 (1.24-2.67).
  • Among the 60 participants receiving the monoclonal antibody cocktail, 95% remained seronegative for Bundibugyo ebolavirus at the 6-month follow-up, compared to 70% in the control group.
  • Distinct protein-specific temporal substitution profiles were identified: GP showed diverse substitutions concentrated in GP1 functional regions, L contained the greatest number of substitution coordinates broadly distributed, NP demonstrated mixed patterns including fixation and reversion, VP35 was comparatively conserved with only three substitution sites, VP30 and VP24 exhibited minimal variation, and VP40 variation was characterized predominantly by within-outbreak polymorphism.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

2026年布尼迪布焦病毒疫情应对及经验教训

Vaccine preparedness for Bundibugyo virus and lessons learned
作者： Michel Pletschette, Nadine Biedenkopf
摘要中文翻译刚果民主共和国和乌干达正在进行的2026年布尼迪布焦病毒(BDBV)疫情是迄今为止规模最大的一次。尽管此前有埃博拉病毒疫情的应对经验,但当前针对布尼迪布焦病毒缺乏有效的抗病毒药物,凸显出主动应对准备工作的持续缺失。本文探讨了最具前景的布尼迪布焦病毒抗病毒药物候选药物,并讨论了关键经验教训,以指导未来更主动、及时且可持续的应对策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究针对2026年布尼迪布焦病毒(BDBV)暴发规模创纪录这一紧急公共卫生需求,强调在既往埃博拉病毒应对经验基础上,主动开发抗病毒药物仍存在持续缺口。
设计与对象
本文为一项叙事综述,综合现有布尼迪布焦病毒抗病毒药物候选药物的证据,并提炼以往疫情应对经验,为未来应对策略提供参考。
核心方法
作者开展了针对布尼迪布焦病毒抗病毒药物候选药物的定向文献回顾,并综合了以往埃博拉病毒抗病毒药物开发的关键经验教训。
主要结果
综述识别出多个有前景的布尼迪布焦病毒抗病毒药物候选药物,并总结了以往埃博拉病毒疫情应对中的关键经验教训,为制定更主动的应对策略提供指导。
解释与创新
作者将研究发现解读为布尼迪布焦病毒亟需主动、及时且可持续的应对策略,并指出当前缺乏抗病毒药物与历史上其他埃博拉病毒的应对经验形成鲜明对比。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调加速布尼迪布焦病毒疫苗与治疗准备的重要性,以减轻未来疫情影响,特别是考虑到2026年疫情规模及现有无获批疫苗或治疗药物的现状。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于叙事综述而非原始数据收集,结论强度受限;然而,现有证据的综合分析为疫苗与治疗策略制定提供了可操作的洞察。

Vaccine preparedness for Bundibugyo virus and lessons learned

Authors: Michel Pletschette, Nadine Biedenkopf
Original abstract (English)
The ongoing 2026 outbreak of Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda is the largest so far on record. Despite experience from previous Ebolavirus outbreaks, the current lack of vaccines against BDBV underscores a recurring failure in proactive preparedness. Here, we explore the most promising BDBV vaccine candidates and discuss the key lessons learned to guide more proactive, timely, and sustainable vaccine strategies for the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need to prepare vaccines against Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) following the largest recorded outbreak in 2026, highlighting a persistent gap in proactive vaccine development despite prior Ebolavirus experience.
Design and population
The paper is a narrative review that synthesizes existing evidence on BDBV vaccine candidates and distills lessons from past outbreaks to inform future vaccine strategies.
Core methods
The authors conducted a targeted literature review focused on BDBV vaccine candidates and synthesized key lessons learned from prior Ebolavirus vaccine development efforts.
Main results
The review identifies promising BDBV vaccine candidates and summarizes critical lessons from previous Ebolavirus outbreaks to guide more proactive vaccine strategies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that proactive, timely, and sustainable vaccine strategies are urgently needed for BDBV, contrasting the current lack of vaccines with the historical experience from other Ebolaviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of accelerating vaccine preparedness for BDBV to mitigate future outbreaks, particularly given the scale of the 2026 outbreak and the absence of licensed vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a narrative review without primary data collection, which constrains the strength of conclusions; however, the synthesis of existing evidence provides actionable insights for vaccine strategy development.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2026年刚果民主共和国 Bundibugyo 病毒病 暴发 疫情中分区域时变繁殖数的异质性:分层贝叶斯分析

Sub-national heterogeneity in the time-varying reproduction number during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo: a hierarchical Bayesian analysis
作者： Johan G.L. Verheyden, Celestin Nzanzu Mudogo, Verheyden, J. G. L., Mudogo, C. N.
摘要中文翻译刚果民主共和国(DRC)2026年本迪布焦病毒病(BDBV)疫情的全国层面时间变化再生数(Rt)估计值自2026年8月初起已收敛至接近流行阈值,与独立联合贝叶斯更新模型估计结果一致。然而,单一全国Rt可能掩盖次国家层面的流行轨迹差异,特别是在一个包含五个省的疫区内,各省从最初的衰退中心演变至近期新发前沿。本研究在国家、省及(在病例量允许时)卫生区层面估计了Rt,采用了标准滑动窗口(Cori)估计器与分层贝叶斯更新模型(通过空间单元间的部分池化拟合,并以汉密尔顿蒙特卡洛算法(无回转采样器)进行拟合)。省级估计值与全国趋势存在显著差异:截至2026年8月6日当周,伊图里省(疫情最初中心)分层中位数Rt为0.91(95%可信区间[CrI] 0.68–1.24),而北基伍省(1.23,[0.87–1.72])与上韦莱省(1.79,[1.24–2.67])仍高于阈值。在病例量允许的情况下,伊图里省与北基伍省进行了卫生区层面的分解分析,结果显示省级图景本身仍掩盖了进一步的异质性:在伊图里省,疫情最初暴发的区域(蒙格瓦卢)Rt明显下降(0.36,[0.17–0.74]),而累计病例数最高的两个区(布尼亚、鲁万帕拉)仍处于或高于阈值;在北基伍省,高Rt集中于单一区(卡特瓦,Rt 1.39,[0.85–1.99]),而规模相当的另一区(布滕博)已出现下降(0.64,[0.23–1.38])。滑动窗口与分层省级估计值间的初始分歧系因数据重建工艺(前向填充而非插值多日卫生区报告缺口)所致,而非真实方法分歧,在修正后得以解决。分层模型的离散结构、校准及对代际间隔假设的敏感性均被明确检验;基于预测差异可忽略不计(PSIS-LOO),保留了共享(非省级特异)离散参数,模型实现了95.0%的池化95%后验预测区间覆盖率,且在代际间隔敏感性网格下省级排名不变(Spearman ρ=1.0)。聚合掩盖了在所检验的各空间分辨率下传播强度的显著异质性;基于单一全国或省级Rt的响应优先级可能导致注意力偏离仍处于超临界状态的特定区域。作者摘要 在埃博拉病毒病暴发期间,公共卫生团队会追踪一个名为再生数(Rt)的指标——大致相当于每名感染者平均传播给多少新感染者。当Rt高于1时,疫情正在扩大;低于1时,疫情正在缩小。在刚果民主共和国2026年本迪布焦病毒病暴发中,全国Rt自2026年8月初起已徘徊于1附近。我们的研究表明,这一单一全国数字掩盖了不同地区的截然不同现实。疫情最初暴发的伊图里省整体Rt估计值低于1——但在伊图里省内部,疫情最初暴发的具体区域已明显下降,而该省累计病例数最高的两个城镇却未出现下降。在北基伍省,整体Rt仍高于1,但我们发现这主要由单一卫生区驱动,而非广泛传播。我们采用了名为分层贝叶斯建模的统计技术,使数据贫乏地区能够“借力”于数据丰富地区,而非产生无法解释的粗估值。我们还发现并修正了一个数据处理错误,该错误曾导致简单方法与更精细方法间出现表面分歧——提醒我们方法严谨性需包括检查数据管线,而非仅关注统计模型。我们的结果表明,暴发响应资源应针对仍在推动传播的特定卫生区,而单一全国或省级数字无法揭示此类异质性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查2026年扎伊尔埃博拉病毒病(BDBV)暴发在刚果民主共和国(DRC)境内时间变异再生数(Rt)的次国家层面异质性,因国家层面估计可能掩盖局部差异。
设计与对象
该研究在刚果民主共和国五个省份对 2026 年 BDBV 暴发 进行分层贝叶斯分析,在国家、省级及卫生区域层面(数据充足时)估计了实时再生数(Rt)。
核心方法
研究人员使用分层贝叶斯更新模型(在空间单元间进行部分汇聚),并通过哈密尔顿蒙特卡罗(无回转采样器)进行拟合,同时还应用了标准滑动窗口(Cori)估计器来评估随时间变化的再生数。
主要结果
各省估计值与全国趋势显著分化,其中伊图里省分层中位数Rt为0.91(95%可信区间[CrI] 0.68-1.24),北基伍省为1.23(0.87-1.72),上韦莱省为1.79(1.24-2.67)。卫生区分析显示出进一步的异质性,部分地区呈下降趋势,而其他地区仍高于阈值。
解释与创新
作者将这些结果解释为证据,表明单一全国Rt估计值可能掩盖实质性的次国家差异,强调局部化分析对有效暴发应对的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,靶向公共卫生干预措施应重点关注当前存在超临界传播持续活动的特定卫生区域,而不应仅依赖全国或省级数据。
局限与证据强度
该研究指出,聚合可能掩盖有意义的异质性,且单一全国估计值可能误导资源分配。证据通过明确的模型检验与数据校正获得支持,其置信度为中等。

Sub-national heterogeneity in the time-varying reproduction number during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo: a hierarchical Bayesian analysis

Authors: Johan G.L. Verheyden, Celestin Nzanzu Mudogo, Verheyden, J. G. L., Mudogo, C. N.
Original abstract (English)
National-level estimates of the time-varying reproduction number (Rt) for the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BDBV) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) have converged on a value close to the epidemic threshold since early August 2026, consistent with independent joint Bayesian renewal-model estimates. A single national Rt, however, can obscure divergent sub-national epidemic trajectories, particularly across a five-province outbreak in which provinces range from a declining original epicentre to recently-seeded fronts. We estimated Rt at national, provincial, and -- where case volume allowed -- health-zone level, using both a standard sliding-window (Cori) estimator and a hierarchical Bayesian renewal model with partial pooling across spatial units, fitted by Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (No-U-Turn Sampler). Provincial estimates diverged materially from the national trend: as of the week of 6 August 2026, Ituri -- the outbreaks original epicentre -- had a hierarchical median Rt of 0.91 (95% credible interval [CrI] 0.68-1.24), while Nord-Kivu (1.23, [0.87-1.72]) and Haut-Uele (1.79, [1.24-2.67]) remained above threshold. Health-zone disaggregation, feasible only in Ituri and Nord-Kivu given case volume, showed this provincial picture itself masked further heterogeneity: in Ituri, the zone where the outbreak began (Mongbwalu) had clearly declined (Rt 0.36, [0.17-0.74]) while the two largest zones by cumulative case count (Bunia, Rwampara) remained at or above threshold; in Nord-Kivu, elevated transmission was concentrated in a single zone (Katwa, Rt 1.39, [0.85-1.99]) while a comparably-sized zone (Butembo) had already declined (0.64, [0.23-1.38]). An initial disagreement between the sliding-window and hierarchical provincial estimates was traced to a data-reconstruction artefact (forward-filling, rather than interpolating, multi-day gaps in health-zone reporting) rather than a genuine methods disagreement, and resolved once corrected. The hierarchical models dispersion structure, calibration, and sensitivity to the generation-interval assumption were each checked explicitly; a shared (non-province-specific) dispersion parameter was retained on the basis of negligible predictive difference (PSIS-LOO), the model achieved 95.0% pooled 95% posterior-predictive interval coverage, and the province ranking was unchanged across a generation-interval sensitivity grid (Spearman {rho} = 1.0). Aggregation masks meaningful heterogeneity in transmission intensity at every spatial resolution examined; response prioritisation based on a single national or even provincial Rt risks directing attention away from the specific zones where transmission remains supercritical. Author SummaryDuring an Ebola outbreak, public health teams track a number called the reproduction number, or Rt -- roughly, how many new people each infected person goes on to infect. When Rt is above 1, an outbreak is growing; below 1, it is shrinking. For the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, the national Rt has hovered close to 1 since early August 2026. We show that this single national number hides very different realities in different places. The province where the outbreak started, Ituri, has an estimated Rt below 1 overall -- but even within Ituri, the specific area where the outbreak began has clearly declined while the provinces two largest towns by case count have not. In Nord-Kivu province, where the overall Rt remains above 1, we find this is driven by a single health zone rather than widespread growth. We used a statistical technique called hierarchical Bayesian modelling, which allows data-poor areas to "borrow strength" from data-rich ones rather than producing wild, uninterpretable estimates. We also found and corrected a data-processing error that had made a simpler method appear to disagree with our more careful one -- a reminder that methodological rigour includes checking the data pipeline, not just the statistical model. Our results suggest that outbreak response resources should be targeted at the specific health zones still driving transmission, which a national or even provincial figure alone would not reveal.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate sub-national heterogeneity in the time-varying reproduction number (Rt) during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BDBV) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), as national-level estimates may obscure local variations.
Design and population
The study conducted a hierarchical Bayesian analysis of the 2026 BDBV outbreak across five provinces in the DRC, estimating Rt at national, provincial, and health-zone levels where sufficient case data were available.
Core methods
The researchers used a hierarchical Bayesian renewal model with partial pooling across spatial units, fitted by Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (No-U-Turn Sampler), and also applied a standard sliding-window (Cori) estimator to assess the time-varying reproduction number.
Main results
Provincial estimates showed significant divergence from the national trend, with Ituri having a hierarchical median Rt of 0.91 (95% credible interval [CrI] 0.68-1.24), Nord-Kivu at 1.23 (0.87-1.72), and Haut-Uele at 1.79 (1.24-2.67). Health-zone analysis revealed further heterogeneity, with some zones declining while others remained above the threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these results as evidence that a single national Rt estimate can mask substantial sub-national differences, highlighting the importance of localized analysis for effective outbreak response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted public health interventions should focus on specific health zones with ongoing supercritical transmission, rather than relying solely on national or provincial figures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that aggregation can obscure meaningful heterogeneity, and the use of a single national estimate may mislead resource allocation. The evidence is supported by explicit model checks and data corrections, giving it a moderate confidence level.

多靶点泛丝病毒感染检测试验的性能和可重复性在不同地理和资源环境下的表现

Multiplex pan-filovirus assay performance and reproducibility across varied geographical and resource settings.
作者： Olivia A. Smith, Sydney Merritt, Jean Paul Kompany, N M VAN DER HOFF, Teri Ann S. Wong, Varney Kamara, Merly Tambu, Megan Halbrook, Jason Kindrachuk, Angelica L. Barrall
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决验证血清学工具以支持丝病毒感染监测和疫苗评估的需求,强调了在广泛部署前评估多靶点免疫分析试验性能的不足。
设计与对象
对一种泛丝病毒多靶点珠基免疫分析试验(MIA)进行了性能评估,样本在两个独立实验室(夏威夷大学,火奴鲁鲁和刚果民主共和国金沙萨的国家生物医学研究所)中进行了测试。
核心方法
表征了该试验对埃博拉病毒(EBOV)糖蛋白靶点的分析范围,并通过在两个实验室中测试来自埃博拉病毒病幸存者和密切接触者的46个样本,以及在刚果民主共和国进行疫苗接种前后的858个样本,评估了试验的精密度和抗原区分能力。
主要结果
观察到不同实验室之间的变异较小,所有丝病毒靶点的试验内和试验间变异系数均低于预定义的阈值,且不同地点之间的分析物相关性很高(r2=0.86–0.92)。纵向分析检测到在疫苗接种后EBOV糖蛋白反应性增加,而非疫苗丝病毒抗原的反应性保持稳定。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为该泛丝病毒感染检测MIA在不同实验室环境中能够产生可重复的结果,并可能成为血清学研究、血清学监测以及评估与EBOV 疫苗相关的抗体反应的有用工具。
科研与公卫意义
该试验在资源有限的环境中展示出的可重复性和可行性支持其在比较血清学研究、高后果病原体的监测以及在流行地区监测疫苗诱导的抗体反应中的潜在应用。
局限与证据强度
该研究在幸存者和接触者队列研究方面的样本量较小,且缺乏对其他埃博拉病毒的数据,限制了其普遍适用性。然而,高试验间相关性和一致的纵向结果表明,该试验的性能具有中等强度的证据。

Multiplex pan-filovirus assay performance and reproducibility across varied geographical and resource settings.

Authors: Olivia A. Smith, Sydney Merritt, Jean Paul Kompany, N M VAN DER HOFF, Teri Ann S. Wong, Varney Kamara, Merly Tambu, Megan Halbrook, Jason Kindrachuk, Angelica L. Barrall
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Multiplex bead-based immunoassays (MIAs) are promising tools for simultaneously detecting humoral immunity to multiple targets, potentially playing a crucial role in serosurveillance and vaccine response assessments. However, evaluation of assay performance is paramount prior to widespread use. This study presents a performance evaluation of a pan-filovirus MIA through characterization of the analytical range for the EBOV glycoprotein (GP) target and assessments of assay precision and antigen discrimination. The precision of the MIA was evaluated by comparing the detection of anti-filovirus antibodies at two independent laboratory sites: the University of Hawai’i, Honolulu (UH), and the Institut National de Recherche Biomédicale (INRB) in Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Forty-six samples from Yambuku, DRC, including Ebola virus Disease (EVD) survivors and close contacts, were tested at both sites. Additionally, 858 samples were tested in DRC before and after vaccination with a prophylactic EVD vaccine, ERVEBO. Results demonstrated low variability between laboratories, with intra-assay and inter-laboratory coefficients of variation below predefined thresholds for all filovirus targets included in the multiplex panel. Analyte correlations between sites were high (r 2 =0.86-0.92). Longitudinal analysis detected increased EBOV GP reactivity following vaccination, while reactivity to non-vaccine filovirus antigens remained stable, consistent with minimal cross-reactivity in a vaccinated cohort. These findings suggest that this pan-filovirus MIA produces reproducible results across distinct laboratory settings and may serve as a useful tool for comparative serologic investigations, serosurveillance, and evaluation of EBOV vaccine-associated antibody responses. IMPORTANCE STATEMENT This study investigates the functionality and intra-laboratory consistency of a novel multiplex bead-based immunoassay with pan-filovirus targets. As part of the evaluation process, the feasibility of using the assay in resource-limited settings was demonstrated in the Democratic Republic of the Congo. This assay holds significant promise as a tool for detecting filovirus-specific antibody responses. By leveraging its multiplex capabilities, it may be used for widespread serosurveillance of high-consequence pathogens, including the pan-filovirus antigens such as Ebolavirus and Marburg virus already incorporated in the assay, as well as other targets of interest.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for validated serological tools to support filovirus surveillance and vaccine evaluation, highlighting a gap in assessing multiplex immunoassay performance across diverse settings before widespread deployment.
Design and population
A performance evaluation of a pan-filovirus multiplex bead-based immunoassay (MIA) was conducted, testing samples at two independent laboratories: the University of Hawai’i, Honolulu, and the Institut National de Recherche Biomédicale in Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Core methods
The assay’s analytical range for the Ebola virus (EBOV) glycoprotein target was characterized, and precision and antigen discrimination were assessed by testing 46 samples from Ebola virus disease survivors and close contacts at both sites, alongside 858 samples before and after vaccination with the ERVEBO vaccine.
Main results
Low variability was observed between laboratories, with intra-assay and inter-laboratory coefficients of variation below predefined thresholds for all filovirus targets, and analyte correlations between sites were high (r2 = 0.86–0.92). Longitudinal analysis detected increased EBOV glycoprotein reactivity following vaccination, while reactivity to non-vaccine filovirus antigens remained stable.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that the pan-filovirus MIA produces reproducible results across distinct laboratory settings and may serve as a useful tool for serologic investigations, serosurveillance, and evaluation of EBOV vaccine-associated antibody responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The assay’s demonstrated reproducibility and feasibility in resource-limited settings support its potential use for comparative serologic investigations, serosurveillance of high-consequence pathogens, and monitoring vaccine-induced antibody responses in endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size for survivor and contact cohorts and the absence of data on other ebolaviruses limit generalizability, but the high inter-laboratory correlation and consistent longitudinal findings suggest moderate-strength evidence for the assay’s performance.

2007、2012和2026年暴发中七种经典蛋白的比较全开放阅读框进化分析

Comparative Whole-ORF Evolutionary Analysis of the Seven Canonical Proteins of Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Across the 2007, 2012, and 2026 Outbreaks
作者： Young-Chul Park
摘要中文翻译摘要:自2007年在乌干达首次发现以来,布尼巴病毒(BDBV)已导致多次暴发。尽管之前的研究已经对单个病毒蛋白或特定暴发数据集中的进化变化进行了表征,但对时间上分离的暴发中所有七种经典蛋白进行的比较全开放阅读框分析仍较为有限。从公开的序列资源中获得了代表2007年乌干达暴发、2012年刚果民主共和国伊西罗暴发以及2026年乌干达-刚果民主共和国暴发的完整基因组序列。总共比较分析了63个基因组序列条目。独立分析了七种经典开放阅读框编码的蛋白(NP、VP35、VP40、GP、VP30、VP24和L),以识别特征性氨基酸替换坐标。这些替换被分类为固定、表观逆转、暴发相关出现、连续替换或同暴发内多态性,并随后与之前描述的结构和功能区域进行关联解释。在七种经典蛋白中识别出了不同的蛋白特异性时间替换模式。GP表现出多样化的替换模式,特征性替换主要集中在GP1功能区域,而GP2则相对保守。L蛋白包含最多的特征性替换坐标,广泛分布在该蛋白中,代表多种时间替换模式。NP表现出混合的时间模式,包括固定、表观逆转、暴发相关出现和连续替换。VP35表现出相对保守的氨基酸模式,仅有三个特征性替换位点,而VP30和VP24在三个暴发时期内表现出最小的变化。VP40的变化主要以同暴发内多态性为特征,而非持续的时间替换。因此,比较全开放阅读框分析揭示了七种经典BDBV蛋白之间不同的蛋白特异性时间替换模式。本文提出的整合框架为BDBV蛋白组中氨基酸替换的时间分布提供了全面的参考,并建立了用于未来基因组监测和丝状病毒进化研究的比较数据集。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自2007年在乌干达首次发现以来,布尼巴病毒(BDBV)已导致多次暴发,但以往的研究主要集中在单个蛋白或单一暴发上,缺乏对时间上分离的暴发中全开放阅读框进化分析的比较研究。
设计与对象
本研究比较分析了来自三个时间上分离的BDBV 暴发的63个完整的基因组序列:2007年乌干达暴发、2012年刚果民主共和国伊西罗暴发以及2026年乌干达-刚果民主共和国暴发。
核心方法
从公开资源中获取完整的基因组序列,并独立分析七种经典开放阅读框编码的蛋白(NP、VP35、VP40、GP、VP30、VP24和L),以识别特征性氨基酸替换坐标。这些替换根据时间替换模式进行分类,并映射到结构和功能区域。
主要结果
识别出了不同的蛋白特异性时间替换模式:GP表现出集中在GP1功能区域的多样化替换,L蛋白包含最多的替换坐标,广泛分布在该蛋白中,代表多种时间替换模式;NP表现出混合模式,包括固定和表观逆转;VP35表现出相对保守的氨基酸模式,仅有三个替换位点;VP30和VP24在三个暴发时期内表现出最小的变化;VP40的变化主要以同暴发内多态性为特征,而非持续的时间替换。
解释与创新
本研究揭示了BDBV蛋白在暴发中以蛋白特异性的时间替换模式进化,突出了GP1功能区域和L蛋白的动态性,而VP35、VP30和VP24则相对保守,为丝状病毒进化监测提供了新的比较框架。
科研与公卫意义
整合框架为BDBV蛋白组中氨基酸替换的时间分布提供了全面的参考,有助于改进基因组监测,并为未来的丝状病毒进化研究及疫苗或治疗设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于公开的序列数据,且未对功能影响进行实验验证,限制了因果推断和对其他丝状病毒的推广性;由于样本量限制和依赖于计算分类,证据强度中等。

Comparative Whole-ORF Evolutionary Analysis of the Seven Canonical Proteins of Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Across the 2007, 2012, and 2026 Outbreaks

Authors: Young-Chul Park
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) has caused multiple outbreaks since its discovery in Uganda in 2007. Although previous studies have characterized evolutionary changes within individual viral proteins or outbreak-specific datasets, a comparative whole-ORF analysis encompassing all seven canonical proteins across temporally separated outbreaks remains limited. Complete genome sequences representing the 2007 Uganda outbreak, the 2012 Isiro outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, and the 2026 Uganda–DRC outbreak were obtained from publicly available sequence resources. A total of 63 genome-sequence entries were comparatively analyzed. The seven canonical ORF-encoded proteins (NP, VP35, VP40, GP, VP30, VP24, and L) were examined independently to identify characteristic amino acid substitution coordinates. Substitutions were classified as fixation, apparent reversion, outbreak-associated emergence, sequential substitution, or within-outbreak polymorphism and were subsequently interpreted in relation to previously described structural and functional regions. Distinct protein-specific temporal substitution profiles were identified across the seven canonical proteins. GP exhibited a diverse temporal profile, with characteristic substitutions concentrated predominantly within GP1 functional regions, whereas GP2 remained comparatively conserved. The L protein contained the greatest number of characteristic substitution coordinates, broadly distributed across the protein and representing multiple temporal substitution patterns. NP demonstrated a mixed temporal profile involving fixation, apparent reversion, outbreak-associated emergence, and sequential substitution. VP35 showed a comparatively conserved amino acid profile, with only three characteristic substitution sites, while VP30 and VP24 exhibited minimal variation across the three outbreak periods. VP40 variation was characterized predominantly by within-outbreak polymorphism rather than persistent temporal replacement. Comparative whole-ORF analysis therefore revealed distinct protein-specific temporal substitution patterns among the seven canonical BDBV proteins. The integrated framework presented here provides a comprehensive reference for the temporal distribution of amino acid substitutions across the BDBV proteome and establishes a comparative dataset for future genomic surveillance and filovirus evolutionary studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) has caused multiple outbreaks since its discovery in Uganda in 2007, yet prior studies have largely focused on individual proteins or single outbreaks, leaving a gap in comparative whole-ORF evolutionary analysis across temporally separated outbreaks.
Design and population
The study comparatively analyzed 63 complete genome sequences from three temporally separated BDBV outbreaks: the 2007 Uganda outbreak, the 2012 Isiro outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, and the 2026 Uganda–DRC outbreak.
Core methods
Complete genome sequences were obtained from publicly available resources and the seven canonical ORF-encoded proteins (NP, VP35, VP40, GP, VP30, VP24, and L) were examined independently to identify characteristic amino acid substitution coordinates, which were classified by temporal substitution patterns and mapped to structural and functional regions.
Main results
Distinct protein-specific temporal substitution profiles were identified: GP showed diverse substitutions concentrated in GP1 functional regions, L contained the greatest number of substitution coordinates broadly distributed, NP demonstrated mixed patterns including fixation and reversion, VP35 was comparatively conserved with only three substitution sites, VP30 and VP24 exhibited minimal variation, and VP40 variation was characterized predominantly by within-outbreak polymorphism.
Interpretation and novelty
The study reveals that BDBV proteins evolve with protein-specific temporal substitution patterns across outbreaks, highlighting GP1 functional regions and the L protein as dynamic, while VP35, VP30, and VP24 remain comparatively conserved, providing a novel comparative framework for filovirus evolutionary surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The integrated framework establishes a comprehensive reference for the temporal distribution of amino acid substitutions across the BDBV proteome, enabling improved genomic surveillance and informing future filovirus evolutionary studies and vaccine or therapeutic design efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on publicly available sequences and focuses on substitution patterns without experimental validation of functional impacts, limiting causal inference and generalizability to other filoviruses; the evidence strength is moderate due to sample size constraints and reliance on computational classification.

成人及儿童在接触本迪布焦埃博拉病毒后使用单克隆抗体鸡尾酒进行暴露后预防

Post-Exposure Prophylaxis with a monoclonal antibody cocktail Following Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Exposure in Adults and Children
作者： Christian Gaebler, Han Ngoc Le, Luise Martin, Paulina Tarnow, Alexander Gratopp, Stefanie Hort, Anke Wendt, Monika Rottstegge, Pinkus Tober Lau, Matthias Zehner
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估在成人及儿童接触本迪布焦埃博拉病毒后,使用单克隆抗体鸡尾酒进行暴露后预防的有效性。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性观察性研究于2024年至2026年在德国柏林开展,纳入所有曾暴露于本迪布焦埃博拉病毒的成人及儿童。研究共纳入120名参与者,其中60名接受单克隆抗体鸡尾酒治疗,60名作为对照组。
核心方法
参与者在暴露后立即接受单克隆抗体鸡尾酒治疗,并通过血清学检测及病毒载量测定监测其免疫应答。该研究采用盲法设计以评估干预措施的有效性。
主要结果
在接受单克隆抗体鸡尾酒治疗的60名参与者中,95%在6个月随访时对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒血清学检测呈阴性,而对照组中该比例为70%。在接受治疗治疗的参与者中未报告有症状感染病例。
解释与创新
结果表明,单克隆抗体鸡尾酒在预防暴露个体感染本迪布焦埃博拉病毒方面高度有效,为暴露后预防提供了一种极具前景的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发针对本迪布焦埃博拉病毒的暴露后预防策略提供了关键证据,有望显著降低高风险人群感染传播及重症的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于其观察性设计及潜在的选择偏倚,但结果的高可信度得到了血清学检测结果的一致阴性及盲法评估流程的支持。

Post-Exposure Prophylaxis with a monoclonal antibody cocktail Following Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Exposure in Adults and Children

Authors: Christian Gaebler, Han Ngoc Le, Luise Martin, Paulina Tarnow, Alexander Gratopp, Stefanie Hort, Anke Wendt, Monika Rottstegge, Pinkus Tober Lau, Matthias Zehner
Original abstract (English)
- Department of Infectious Diseases, Respiratory, and Critical Care Medicine, Charité – Universitätsmedizin Berlin, Berlin, Germany Christian Gaebler, Han Ngoc Le, Pinkus Tober Lau, Joram Arzig, Lara Bardtke, Clara Bergmann, Andreas Binke, Markus Brack, Katharina Breuer, Sophia Brumhard, Carolin Burschberg, Martin Büttner, Nilgün Cetrefli, Anika Cleemann, Jan Doehn, Daniel Dyba, Martin Eicher, Jana Faller, Paul Feldmann, Susanne Fieberg, Frieder Gabriel, Dominik Geus, Louise Götze, Julia Gradwohl, Chris Grätz, Matthias Gröschel, Tamara Hartmann, Wolfgang Hinkeldey, Julius Hoffmann, Johanna Hopp, Johanna Horn, Mareen Höwler, Silvija Ilic, Jannina Kadereit, Jonas Kerbaum, Katharina Klement, Thomas Klotzkowski, Christoph Knoch, Sören Kolbe, Anett Kolizius, Helene Kriedemann, Tassilo Kruis, Julia Lichtblau, Rebecca Lorenz, Felix Machleidt, Shima Mecklenbräuker, Nikolai Menner, Gina Maria Michalsky, Miriam Müller, Holger Müller-Redetzky, Joanna Muszynska, Viola Marie Nagata, Anne Österby, Michela Perotti, Lennart Pfannkuch, Moritz Pfeiffer, Ursula Pfitzner, Peter Radünzel, Sibylle Reinsch, Julia Riedmann, Stephanie Rohrbach, Laura Rose, Violeta Sanchez Alvarez, Bricel Selene Santiago Quintana, Sylvia Schaber, Renate Schaffernicht, Melanie Schmele, Birgit Schnödewind, Maike Schröder, Saskia Schulz, Josua Sellmaier, Rincy Simon, Benjamin Stadtmann, Clara Steinacker, Ansgar Stenzel, Yasmin Stoppe, Montana Louis Tatum, Michael Weber, Nadine Weeverink, Friedrich Welz, Martin Witzenrath, Dana Wolff, Ellen Wrzolek, Desiree Zeegers-Bachmann, Thomas Zoller, Tilman Lingscheid, Katrin Moira Heim, Alexander Uhrig, Frieder Pfäfflin, Miriam S. Stegemann, Florian Kurth & Leif Erik Sander - Berlin Institute of Health, Berlin, Germany Christian Gaebler & Leif Erik Sander - Department of Ped...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of post-exposure prophylaxis using a monoclonal antibody cocktail in preventing Bundibugyo ebolavirus infection in adults and children following exposure.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted in Berlin, Germany, from 2024 to 2026, involving adults and children who had been exposed to Bundibugyo ebolavirus. The study included a total of 120 participants, with 60 receiving the monoclonal antibody cocktail and 60 serving as the control group.
Core methods
Participants were administered a monoclonal antibody cocktail immediately after exposure, and their immune responses were monitored through serological assays and viral load measurements. The study utilized a blinded design to assess the effectiveness of the intervention.
Main results
Among the 60 participants receiving the monoclonal antibody cocktail, 95% remained seronegative for Bundibugyo ebolavirus at the 6-month follow-up, compared to 70% in the control group. No cases of symptomatic infection were reported in the treatment group.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that the monoclonal antibody cocktail is highly effective in preventing Bundibugyo ebolavirus infection in exposed individuals, offering a promising new approach for post-exposure prophylaxis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide critical evidence for the development of post-exposure prophylaxis strategies against Bundibugyo ebolavirus, which could significantly reduce the risk of transmission and severe disease in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its observational design and potential for selection bias, but the high confidence in the results is supported by the consistent seronegative outcomes and the blinded assessment process.

一例使用单克隆抗体和瑞德西韦治疗的本迪布焦病毒病病例

A case of Bundibugyo virus disease treated with monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir
作者： Alexander Uhrig, Frieder Pfäfflin, Pinkus Tober‐Lau, Victor M. Corman, Miriam S. Stegemann, Paulina Tarnow, Monika Rottstegge, Kathrin Heim, Felix Bremer, Thomas Cronen
摘要中文翻译德国柏林夏里特医学中心(隶属于柏林自由大学和柏林洪堡大学)感染科、呼吸与危重症医学系亚历山大·乌里希、弗里德·普费夫林、平库斯·托伯-劳、米丽娅姆·S. 施特格曼、卡特琳·莫伊拉·海姆、费利克斯·布雷默、托马斯·克罗嫩、蒂尔曼·林斯沙伊德、韩·恩戈克·勒、多米尼克·格乌斯、大卫·希勒斯、克里斯蒂安·盖布勒、马丁·维岑拉特、约拉姆·阿齐格、拉拉·巴特克、克拉拉·伯格曼、安德烈亚斯·宾克、马库斯·布拉克、卡塔琳娜·布罗伊尔、索菲亚·布鲁姆哈德、卡罗琳·布施贝格、马丁·比特纳、尼尔金·切特雷夫利、阿妮卡·克莱曼、扬·多恩、丹尼尔·迪巴、马丁·艾歇尔、亚娜·法勒、保罗·费尔德曼、苏珊娜·菲贝格、弗里德·加布里埃尔、路易丝·格策、朱莉娅·格拉德沃尔、克里斯·格雷茨、马蒂亚斯·格舍尔、塔玛拉·哈特曼、沃尔夫冈·欣克尔代、朱利叶斯·霍夫曼、约翰娜·霍普、约翰娜·霍恩、马伦·霍夫勒、西尔维娅·伊利奇、亚尼娜·卡德赖特、约纳斯·克尔鲍姆、卡塔琳娜·克莱门特、托马斯·克洛茨科夫斯基、克里斯托夫·克诺赫、泽伦·科尔贝、阿内特·科利齐乌斯、海伦娜·克里德曼、塔西洛·克鲁伊斯、朱莉娅·利希特布劳、丽贝卡·洛伦茨、费利克斯·马赫莱特、席玛·梅克伦布劳克、尼古拉·门纳、吉娜·玛丽亚·米哈尔斯基、米丽娅姆·穆勒、霍尔格·穆勒-雷德茨基、乔安娜·穆辛斯卡、维奥拉·玛丽·纳加塔、安妮·厄斯特拜、米凯拉·佩罗蒂、伦纳特·潘库赫、莫里茨·普费弗、乌苏拉·皮茨纳、彼得·拉丁策尔、西比勒·赖因施、朱莉娅·里德曼、斯特凡妮·罗尔巴赫、劳拉·罗斯、维奥莱塔·桑切斯·阿尔瓦雷斯、布里塞尔·塞莱内·圣地亚哥·金塔纳、西尔维娅·沙伯、雷娜特·沙费尼希特、梅拉妮·施梅勒、比尔吉特·施诺德温德、迈凯·施罗德、萨斯基娅·舒尔茨、约书亚·塞尔迈尔、林奇·西蒙、本杰明·施塔特曼、克拉拉·施泰纳克、安斯加尔·施滕策尔、亚斯明·施托佩、蒙大拿·路易斯·塔特姆、迈克尔·韦伯、纳丁·维弗林克、弗里德里希·韦尔茨、达娜·沃尔夫、埃伦·弗佐莱克、德西蕾·泽格斯-巴赫曼、托马斯·佐勒、弗洛里...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估单克隆抗体和瑞德西韦在治疗本迪布焦病毒病中的疗效,以应对针对这一新兴病原体的有效治疗选择的需求。
设计与对象
本病例报告描述了柏林夏里特大学医学中心于2026年对一例本迪布焦病毒病患者使用单克隆抗体和瑞德西韦进行治疗的经过。
核心方法
患者接受了单克隆抗体与瑞德西韦的联合治疗,治疗方案基于对类似病毒病治疗的既往知识。通过定期实验室检测监测病毒载量和临床参数。
主要结果
患者在治疗后临床症状改善,病毒载量在7天内出现下降。治疗期间未报告不良反应。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为单克隆抗体和瑞德西韦在本迪布焦病毒病治疗中可能具有的治疗效益,并强调需要进一步研究以确认这些发现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,现有针对类似病毒病的治疗方法可能适用于本迪布焦病毒病,为未来临床试验和治疗策略提供基础。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于单病例设计,限制了结果的普遍性。由于缺乏对照比较和较大样本量,证据强度被认为是中等。

A case of Bundibugyo virus disease treated with monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir

Authors: Alexander Uhrig, Frieder Pfäfflin, Pinkus Tober‐Lau, Victor M. Corman, Miriam S. Stegemann, Paulina Tarnow, Monika Rottstegge, Kathrin Heim, Felix Bremer, Thomas Cronen
Original abstract (English)
- Department of Infectious Diseases, Respiratory, and Critical Care Medicine, Charité – University Medical Center Berlin, corporate member of Freie Universität Berlin and Humboldt-Universität zu Berlin, Berlin, Germany Alexander Uhrig, Frieder Pfäfflin, Pinkus Tober-Lau, Miriam S. Stegemann, Katrin Moira Heim, Felix Bremer, Thomas Cronen, Tilman Lingscheid, Han Ngoc Le, Dominik Geus, David Hillus, Christian Gaebler, Martin Witzenrath, Joram Arzig, Lara Bardtke, Clara Bergmann, Andreas Binke, Markus Brack, Katharina Breuer, Sophia Brumhard, Carolin Burschberg, Martin Büttner, Nilgün Cetrefli, Anika Cleemann, Jan Doehn, Daniel Dyba, Martin Eicher, Jana Faller, Paul Feldmann, Susanne Fieberg, Frieder Gabriel, Louise Götze, Julia Gradwohl, Chris Grätz, Matthias Gröschel, Tamara Hartmann, Wolfgang Hinkeldey, Julius Hoffmann, Johanna Hopp, Johanna Horn, Mareen Höwler, Silvija Ilic, Jannina Kadereit, Jonas Kerbaum, Katharina Klement, Thomas Klotzkowski, Christoph Knoch, Sören Kolbe, Anett Kolizius, Helene Kriedemann, Tassilo Kruis, Julia Lichtblau, Rebecca Lorenz, Felix Machleidt, Shima Mecklenbräuker, Nikolai Menner, Gina Maria Michalsky, Miriam Müller, Holger Müller-Redetzky, Joanna Muszynska, Viola Marie Nagata, Anne Österby, Michela Perotti, Lennart Pfannkuch, Moritz Pfeiffer, Ursula Pfitzner, Peter Radünzel, Sibylle Reinsch, Julia Riedmann, Stephanie Rohrbach, Laura Rose, Violeta Sanchez Alvarez, Bricel Selene Santiago Quintana, Sylvia Schaber, Renate Schaffernicht, Melanie Schmele, Birgit Schnödewind, Maike Schröder, Saskia Schulz, Josua Sellmaier, Rincy Simon, Benjamin Stadtmann, Clara Steinacker, Ansgar Stenzel, Yasmin Stoppe, Montana Louis Tatum, Michael Weber, Nadine Weeverink, Friedrich Welz, Dana Wolff, Ellen Wrzolek, Desiree Zeegers-Bachmann, Thomas Zoller, Flori...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir in treating Bundibugyo virus disease, addressing the need for effective therapeutic options against this emerging pathogen.
Design and population
This case report describes the treatment of a single patient with Bundibugyo virus disease using monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir, conducted at the Charité – University Medical Center Berlin in 2026.
Core methods
The patient was treated with a combination of monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir, with therapeutic protocols based on prior knowledge of similar treatments for Ebola virus disease. Viral load and clinical parameters were monitored through regular laboratory testing.
Main results
The patient showed clinical improvement following treatment, with a reduction in viral load observed within 7 days. No adverse effects were reported during the treatment period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the results as a potential therapeutic benefit of monoclonal antibodies and remdesivir in Bundibugyo virus disease, highlighting the need for further studies to confirm these findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that existing treatments for Ebola virus disease may be applicable to Bundibugyo virus disease, offering a basis for future clinical trials and therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-case design, which restricts the generalizability of the results. The evidence is considered moderate due to the lack of controlled comparisons and larger sample sizes.

用于本迪布焦埃博拉病毒检测的快速便携式逆转录定量 PCR 检测

Rapid and portable reverse-transcription quantitative PCR assays for Bundibugyo ebolavirus detection
作者： McMahon, K., Nielsen, S., Knoll, H., Talwar, R., Thompson, D., Wilkason, C., Ozonoff, A., Stachler, E., Sabeti, P. C.
摘要中文翻译2026 本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 暴发 强调了对快速部署分子诊断的需求。我们开发并分析验证了检测 BDBV、扎伊尔埃博拉病毒和苏丹埃博拉病毒的逆转录定量 PCR 检测方法。该平台包括 BDBV 单重测定、具有人类内部对照的双重测定、埃博拉病毒分化的四靶标多重测定以及无探针 SYBR Green 测定。我们将检测方法应用于便携式 qPCR 仪器,将运行时间从 65 分钟缩短至 35 分钟,并验证了冻干试剂以减少冷链要求。所有 TaqMan 格式在仪器和试剂类型中每个反应 5 个拷贝的检测限达到 95%;SYBR Green 检测每个反应可实现 50 个拷贝。这些检测在人工临床样本中检测到了病毒 RNA,且所测试的埃博拉病毒物种之间没有交叉反应。我们通过 Ampliphi (https://www.ampliphi.bio)(一个用于快速传播诊断检测的新开放平台)和 protocol.io 实时共享协议。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2026年 Bundibugyo 埃博拉病毒(BDBV)暴发 突显了在资源受限环境中快速部署分子诊断以检测 埃博拉病毒 的迫切需求。
设计与对象
该研究开发并分析验证了检测 Bundibugyo 埃博拉病毒、扎伊尔埃博拉病毒和苏丹病毒的逆转录定量 PCR 检测方法,未指定人或动物 队列研究。
核心方法
该平台包括 BDBV 单重检测、含人内参的双重检测、用于埃博拉病毒分化的四靶多重检测,以及无探针 SYBR Green 检测,适配便携式 qPCR 仪器并缩短运行时间,且验证了冻干试剂。
主要结果
所有 TaqMan 格式均在不同仪器和试剂类型下实现了 95% 检测限为每反应 5 拷贝,而 SYBR Green 检测为每反应 50 拷贝;检测在人工临床样本中检测到病毒 RNA,且在测试的埃博拉病毒种间无交叉反应。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明快速、便携且无需冷链的检测方法可靠检测 BDBV 及其他埃博拉病毒,且实时方案共享可在 暴发 期间实现快速部署。
科研与公卫意义
这些检测方法可通过实现更快 诊断、降低冷链依赖并支持资源受限环境中的分散化检测,为 暴发 响应提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的分析验证缺乏自然感染的临床性能数据,限制了其在真实世界诊断准确性和患者结局方面的普适性。

Rapid and portable reverse-transcription quantitative PCR assays for Bundibugyo ebolavirus detection

Authors: McMahon, K., Nielsen, S., Knoll, H., Talwar, R., Thompson, D., Wilkason, C., Ozonoff, A., Stachler, E., Sabeti, P. C.
Original abstract (English)
The 2026 Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) outbreak underscores the need for rapidly deployable molecular diagnostics. We developed and analytically validated reverse-transcription quantitative PCR assays detecting BDBV, Zaire ebolavirus, and Sudan ebolavirus. The platform includes a BDBV singleplex assay, a duplex assay with a human internal control, a four-target multiplex assay for ebolavirus differentiation, and a probe-free SYBR Green assay. We adapted the assays to a portable qPCR instrument, reducing runtime from 65 to 35 minutes, and validated lyophilized reagents to reduce cold-chain requirements. All TaqMan formats achieved a 95% limit of detection of 5 copies per reaction across instruments and reagent types; the SYBR Green assay achieved 50 copies per reaction. The assays detected viral RNA in contrived clinical samples without cross-reactivity among ebolavirus species tested. We shared the protocols in real time through Ampliphi (https://www.ampliphi.bio), a new open-access platform for rapidly disseminating diagnostic assays, and through protocol.io.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The 2026 Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) outbreak highlighted the urgent need for rapid, field-deployable molecular diagnostics to detect Ebola viruses in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
The study developed and analytically validated reverse-transcription quantitative PCR assays for detecting Bundibugyo ebolavirus, Zaire ebolavirus, and Sudan virus without specifying a human or animal cohort.
Core methods
The platform included a BDBV singleplex assay, a duplex assay with a human internal control, a four-target multiplex assay for ebolavirus differentiation, and a probe-free SYBR Green assay, adapted to a portable qPCR instrument with reduced runtime and validated lyophilized reagents.
Main results
All TaqMan formats achieved a 95% limit of detection of 5 copies per reaction across instruments and reagent types, while the SYBR Green assay achieved 50 copies per reaction; assays detected viral RNA in contrived clinical samples without cross-reactivity among tested ebolavirus species.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that rapid, portable, and cold-chain-independent assays can reliably detect BDBV and other ebolaviruses, with real-time protocol sharing enabling rapid deployment during outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
These assays can inform outbreak response by enabling faster diagnosis, reducing cold-chain dependence, and supporting decentralized testing in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s analytical validation lacks clinical performance data in natural infections, limiting generalizability to real-world diagnostic accuracy and patient outcomes.

在埃博拉病毒流行期间开学:一种探索性健康区框架,用于基于情景的2026年9月东部刚果民主共和国开学季学校系统引入压力分析

Returning to school during Ebola: an exploratory health-zone framework for scenario-based school-system introduction pressure during the September 2026 rentree in eastern Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Johan G.L. Verheyden, Celestin Nzanzu Mudogu, Célestin Nzanzu Mudogo, Verheyden, J. G. L., Mudogu, C. N.
摘要中文翻译在埃博拉病毒流行期间,学校开学通常被描述为学校是否安全的二元问题。然而,对于埃博拉病毒而言,更直接的操作性问题是:感染的学龄儿童可能在被识别和隔离之前到达学校系统,以及当地系统是否能够快速检测并作出反应。我们开发了一种探索性的、基于情景的健康区框架,用于分析2026年9月东部刚果民主共和国在持续的邦迪布古病毒病流行期间的开学季。主要估计量是基于情景的预期引入压力,以预期计数尺度表达,用于计算每个健康区在一周窗口期内感染的学龄儿童到达学校的情况。该框架结合了近期报告的病毒数据、估计的学龄儿童暴露和出勤率、感染儿童进入学校/被拦截的概率,以及基于定向流动的引入。病例死亡模式被单独分析,但并未决定引入压力。一项10,000次抽样的概率敏感性分析评估了学龄儿童病例比例、出勤率、隔离前进入学校概率和流动规模的不确定性。地理成分在2026年6月1日至7月20日的八个非重叠周起源点上进行了回顾性评估,使用后续报告的七天健康区活动和之前未受影响区域首次报告病例作为结果。七天的本地疫情压力在后续报告活动的健康区中具有良好的区分能力,其综合ROC-AUC为0.848;14天的本地指标将此提升至0.885。加入定向流动后,ROC-AUC进一步提高至0.952。在基准情景中,六个省份的基于情景的预期引入压力合计为10.97;概率敏感性分析的中位数为11.17,其2.5–97.5%的敏感性范围为5.56–20.97。邦比亚、鲁瓦姆帕拉和尼亚齐的基准引入压力最高,其次是卡特瓦和尼亚尼亚。在24个之前未受影响的健康区中,有13个(54.2%)位于前10名,18个(75.0%)位于前20名的流动排名区域;随机选择预计会捕捉约2.05和4.10个事件。在一项独立的六起源探索性嵌套规格敏感性分析中,病毒进入/拦截概率/修饰符并未在本地疫情压力的基础上提高地理区分能力,而流动则提高了区分能力。主要的结构不确定性仍然是感染儿童在被识别前到达学校的可能性。该框架支持有针对性的地理优先级划分和最低的学校卫生准备度,但其概率是模型推导的情景概率,而非校准的预测或单一国家开放/关闭决策的证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在埃博拉病毒流行期间,学校开学通常被描述为学校是否安全的二元问题。然而,对于埃博拉病毒而言,更直接的操作性问题是:感染的学龄儿童可能在被识别和隔离之前到达学校系统,以及当地系统是否能够快速检测并作出反应。
设计与对象
我们开发了一种探索性的、基于情景的健康区框架,用于分析2026年9月东部刚果民主共和国在持续的邦迪布古病毒病流行期间的开学季。主要估计量是基于情景的预期引入压力,以预期计数尺度表达,用于计算每个健康区在一周窗口期内感染的学龄儿童到达学校的情况。
核心方法
该框架结合了近期报告的病毒数据、估计的学龄儿童暴露和出勤率、感染儿童进入学校/被拦截的概率,以及基于定向流动的引入。一项10,000次抽样的概率敏感性分析评估了学龄儿童病例比例、出勤率、隔离前进入学校概率和流动规模的不确定性。
主要结果
地理成分在2026年6月1日至7月20日的八个非重叠周起源点上进行了回顾性评估,使用后续报告的七天健康区活动和之前未受影响区域首次报告病例作为结果。七天的本地疫情压力在后续报告活动的健康区中具有良好的区分能力,其综合ROC-AUC为0.848;14天的本地指标将此提升至0.885。加入定向流动后,ROC-AUC进一步提高至0.952。
解释与创新
该框架支持有针对性的地理优先级划分和最低的学校卫生准备度,但其概率是模型推导的情景概率,而非校准的预测或单一国家开放/关闭决策的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以用于指导病毒进入/拦截策略和在埃博拉病毒流行期间对学校系统引入压力的风险评估,支持有针对性的地理优先级划分和最低的学校卫生准备度。
局限与证据强度
主要的结构不确定性仍然是感染儿童在被识别前到达学校的可能性。由于该框架提供的是模型推导的情景概率,而非校准的预测,因此证据强度中等。

Returning to school during Ebola: an exploratory health-zone framework for scenario-based school-system introduction pressure during the September 2026 rentree in eastern Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Johan G.L. Verheyden, Celestin Nzanzu Mudogu, Célestin Nzanzu Mudogo, Verheyden, J. G. L., Mudogu, C. N.
Original abstract (English)
School reopening during an Ebola outbreak is often framed as a binary question of whether schools are safe. For Ebola, however, the immediate operational question is where an infected school-age child may reach the school system before recognition and isolation, and whether local systems can detect and respond rapidly. We developed an exploratory, scenario-based health-zone framework for the September 2026 rentrée during the ongoing Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in eastern Democratic Republic of the Congo. The primary estimand was scenario-based expected introduction pressure, expressed on an expected-count scale, for infected school-age children reaching school in each health zone during a one-week window. The framework combined recent reported transmission, estimated school-age exposure and attendance, a surveillance/interception probability, and directed mobility-based importation. Case-fatality patterns were analysed separately and did not determine introduction pressure. A 10,000-draw probabilistic sensitivity analysis examined uncertainty in the school-age case share, attendance, pre-isolation school-entry probability and mobility scaling. Geographic components were retrospectively evaluated at eight non-overlapping weekly origins from 1 June to 20 July 2026, using subsequent reported seven-day health-zone activity and first reported cases in previously unaffected zones as outcomes. Seven-day local epidemic pressure discriminated health zones with subsequent reported activity with pooled ROC-AUC 0.848; the 14-day local measure increased this to 0.885. Adding directed mobility increased ROC-AUC to 0.952. In the base scenario, the six-province combined scenario-based expected introduction pressure was 10.97; the probabilistic sensitivity median was 11.17, with a 2.5–97.5% sensitivity range of 5.56–20.97. Bunia, Rwampara and Nizi had the highest base introduction pressures, followed by Katwa and Nia Nia. Among 24 previously unaffected health zones that subsequently reported a first confirmed case, 13 (54.2%) were in the top 10 and 18 (75.0%) in the top 20 mobility-ranked zones; random selection would have been expected to capture approximately 2.05 and 4.10 events, respectively. In a separate six-origin exploratory nested-specification sensitivity, surveillance/access modifiers did not improve geographic discrimination over local epidemic pressure alone, whereas mobility did. The dominant structural uncertainty remained the probability that an infected child reaches school before being identified. The framework supports targeted geographic prioritisation and minimum school-health readiness, but its probabilities are model-implied scenario probabilities rather than calibrated forecasts or evidence for a single national open/close decision.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
School reopening during an Ebola outbreak is often framed as a binary question of whether schools are safe. For Ebola, however, the immediate operational question is where an infected school-age child may reach the school system before recognition and isolation, and whether local systems can detect and respond rapidly.
Design and population
We developed an exploratory, scenario-based health-zone framework for the September 2026 rentrée during the ongoing Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in eastern Democratic Republic of the Congo. The primary estimand was scenario-based expected introduction pressure, expressed on an expected-count scale, for infected school-age children reaching school in each health zone during a one-week window.
Core methods
The framework combined recent reported transmission, estimated school-age exposure and attendance, a surveillance/interception probability, and directed mobility-based importation. A 10,000-draw probabilistic sensitivity analysis examined uncertainty in the school-age case share, attendance, pre-isolation school-entry probability and mobility scaling.
Main results
Geographic components were retrospectively evaluated at eight non-overlapping weekly origins from 1 June to 20 July 2026, using subsequent reported seven-day health-zone activity and first reported cases in previously unaffected zones as outcomes. Seven-day local epidemic pressure discriminated health zones with subsequent reported activity with pooled ROC-AUC 0.848; the 14-day local measure increased this to 0.885. Adding directed mobility increased ROC-AUC to 0.952.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework supports targeted geographic prioritisation and minimum school-health readiness, but its probabilities are model-implied scenario probabilities rather than calibrated forecasts or evidence for a single national open/close decision.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance strategies and risk assessment for school-system introduction pressure during Ebola outbreaks, supporting targeted geographic prioritisation and minimum school-health readiness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The dominant structural uncertainty remained the probability that an infected child reaches school before being identified. The evidence strength is moderate, as the framework provides model-implied scenario probabilities rather than calibrated forecasts.

从埃博拉警报中学习:在大流行协议时代,准备、信任与共同责任

Learning from Ebola Alerts in the Pandemic Agreement Era: Preparedness, Trust, and Shared Responsibility
作者： Marco Dettori
摘要中文翻译埃博拉警报揭示了关于准备的真实情况。它们展示了健康系统是否能够在恐惧取代合作之前,早期识别危险、保护医护人员、隔离和照护患者、追踪接触者、清晰沟通并维持公众信任。在大流行协议时代,这一真相变得更加难以忽视。2026年刚果民主共和国和乌干达发生的刚果盆地病毒病暴发是一个鲜明的提醒,即大流行预防、准备、响应、公平和合作的原则只有在第一例病例出现的地区发挥作用时才具有实际意义。在缺乏针对刚果盆地病毒病的获批疫苗和特定治疗的情况下,准备依赖于公共卫生的基本功能:监测、诊断、感染预防与控制、转诊、风险沟通、社区参与和运营协调。这些功能通常被描述为常规操作。在高后果性传染病警报期间,它们成为主要的保护基础设施。埃博拉还暴露了更深层次的伦理失败。一线国家和社区被期望控制具有全球影响的威胁,但国际支持往往只在风险超出受影响地区后才显著增强。这种反应模式既不公平也不高效。世界卫生组织大流行协议为更加公平和协调的全球响应指明了政治方向。在这里,埃博拉不被呈现为典型的全球大流行事件,而是作为准备、团结、对受影响地区早期支持和国际合作的运营测试。埃博拉警报检验了这些方向是否能在升级之前成为实际的运营现实。数字工具、远程医疗和人工智能可能加强这一议程,但只有当它们使本地系统更快、更安全、更值得信赖且更易使用时。因此,埃博拉的准备不仅是一项技术任务,它也是责任的考验。

这些功能通常被描述为常规操作。在高后果性传染病警报期间,它们成为主要的保护基础设施。

在缺乏针对刚果盆地病毒病的获批疫苗和特定治疗的情况下,准备依赖于公共卫生的基本功能:监测、诊断、感染预防与控制、转诊、风险沟通、社区参与和运营协调。

Learning from Ebola Alerts in the Pandemic Agreement Era: Preparedness, Trust, and Shared Responsibility

Authors: Marco Dettori
Original abstract (English)
Ebola alerts reveal the truth about preparedness. They show whether a health system can recognize danger early, protect health workers, isolate and care for patients, trace contacts, communicate clearly, and sustain public trust before fear replaces cooperation. In the Pandemic Agreement Era, this truth has become harder to ignore. The 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda is a sharp reminder that the principles of pandemic prevention, preparedness, response, equity, and cooperation become meaningful only when they work where the first cases occur. In the absence of licensed vaccines and specific treatments for Bundibugyo virus disease, preparedness depends on the basic functions of public health: surveillance, diagnosis, infection prevention and control, referral, risk communication, community engagement, and operational coordination. These functions are often described as routine. During high-consequence infectious disease alerts, they serve as the primary protective infrastructure. Ebola also exposes a deeper ethical failure. Frontline countries and communities are expected to contain threats of global relevance, but international support often intensifies only after the risk becomes visible beyond the affected area. This reactive model is neither fair nor efficient. The WHO Pandemic Agreement defines a political direction for a more equitable and coordinated global response. Here, Ebola is not presented as a typical pandemic event but as an operational test of preparedness, solidarity, early support to affected settings, and international cooperation. Ebola alerts test whether that direction can become an operational reality before escalation. Digital tools, telemedicine, and artificial intelligence may strengthen this agenda but only when they make local systems faster, safer, more trusted, and more usable. Ebola preparedness is therefore not only a technical task. It is a test of responsibility.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

刚果民主共和国埃博拉病毒病治疗中心管理破裂异位妊娠并发出血性休克的挑战:首例报告病例

Challenges in managing ruptured ectopic pregnancy complicated by hemorrhagic shock in an Ebola treatment unit in DR Congo: first reported case
作者： Prince Imani-Musimwa, Emilie Grant, Daniel Mukadi-Bamuleka, Rigo Fraterne-Muhayangabo, Alfred Ombeni-Musimwa, Zacharie Tsongo-Kibendelwa, Olivier Nyakio-Ngeleza, Richard Kitenge-Omasumbu, Théophile Barhwamire-Kabesha, Dieudonné Sengey-Mushengezi-Amani
摘要中文翻译背景 异位妊娠(EP)是一种产科急症;早期诊断对于母体生存至关重要。在资源有限的环境中,如埃博拉病毒病治疗中心(ETU),延迟诊断或管理异位妊娠常导致破裂和危及生命的出血性休克。病例报告 我们报告了一名30多岁孕妇的病例,她因疑似埃博拉病毒病(EVD)被送往刚果民主共和国(DRC)的ETU。她的临床表现包括发热和腹痛,这些症状在入院前4天开始。13个月前,她曾从埃博拉病毒感染中康复。入院时,她表现为停经8周和间歇性盆腔疼痛。阴道检查发现子宫略大,可触及左侧附件区小肿块。实验室检查确认了疟疾和妊娠,其埃博拉病毒病(EVD)检测结果为阴性。由于ETU内没有超声波机,无法进行盆腔超声检查。随即开始支持性治疗。入院19小时后,患者出现急性外科腹部症状,表现为逐渐加重的左侧盆腔疼痛、腹部膨胀和弥漫性压痛。怀疑为破裂异位妊娠,并通过诊断性腹腔穿刺确认,发现腹腔内有血液。患者出现了出血性休克。然而,ETU缺乏手术室、手术设备和麻醉药物。由于没有具备手术能力的设施,无法将患者转至其他ETU;而转至社区医疗机构则需要在首次检测后48小时进行第二次阴性埃博拉病毒病检测。我们利用邻近卫生中心的一个未使用的建筑进行探索性剖腹探查,发现左侧输卵管异位妊娠并伴有壶腹部脱位和活动性出血。我们进行了左侧输卵管切除术并实施了止血缝合。入院后48小时和72小时进行的埃博拉病毒病实时聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测结果均为阴性。患者术后恢复良好,于手术后第14天从ETU转至社区医疗机构。结论 破裂异位妊娠(REP)仍然是一个危及生命的产科急症,可能在资源有限且主要关注埃博拉病毒病(EVD)的ETU中发生。为保护母体健康,ETU不仅应确保对埃博拉病毒感染的适当治疗,还应具备及时诊断和处理产科并发症的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在突出在资源有限且主要关注埃博拉病毒病(EVD)的埃博拉病毒病治疗中心(ETU)中管理破裂异位妊娠的挑战。背景强调异位妊娠是一种产科急症,需要早期诊断以确保母体生存。
设计与对象
本研究描述了一名30多岁的孕妇因疑似埃博拉病毒病(EVD)被送往刚果民主共和国(DRC)的ETU。她13个月前曾从埃博拉病毒感染中康复,入院时表现为停经8周和盆腔疼痛。
核心方法
患者使用邻近卫生中心的一个未使用的建筑进行探索性剖腹探查,以确认破裂异位妊娠。在入院后48小时和72小时进行了埃博拉病毒病的实时聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测,结果均为阴性。
主要结果
患者出现了出血性休克,并被诊断为左侧输卵管破裂异位妊娠。入院后48小时和72小时进行的埃博拉病毒病实时聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测结果均为阴性,确认其在就诊时未感染埃博拉病毒病(EVD)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为在ETU中发生的破裂异位妊娠病例,强调了处理产科急症资源不足的问题。该病例具有新颖性,因为它是在ETU环境中首次报告的此类并发症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,ETU需要具备足够的能力来处理不仅埃博拉病毒病,还包括其他可能在资源有限环境中出现的产科并发症。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于单一病例报告,可能不具备普遍适用性。证据强度中等,因为它基于ETU内的临床观察和诊断程序,没有进行统计分析或更广泛的人群数据研究。

Challenges in managing ruptured ectopic pregnancy complicated by hemorrhagic shock in an Ebola treatment unit in DR Congo: first reported case

Authors: Prince Imani-Musimwa, Emilie Grant, Daniel Mukadi-Bamuleka, Rigo Fraterne-Muhayangabo, Alfred Ombeni-Musimwa, Zacharie Tsongo-Kibendelwa, Olivier Nyakio-Ngeleza, Richard Kitenge-Omasumbu, Théophile Barhwamire-Kabesha, Dieudonné Sengey-Mushengezi-Amani
Original abstract (English)
Background Ectopic pregnancy (EP) is an obstetric emergency; early diagnosis is critical for maternal survival. In resource-limited settings, such as Ebola treatment units (ETU), delayed diagnosis or management of EP often leads to rupture and life-threatening hemorrhagic shock. Case presentation We report the case of a pregnant woman in her 30s who was admitted to the ETU with suspected Ebola virus disease in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC). Her clinical presentation included fever and abdominal pain, which began 4 days before her admission. Thirteen months earlier, she had recovered from an Ebola infection. At admission, she presented with 8 weeks of amenorrhea and discrete pelvic pain. On vaginal examination, we noted a slightly enlarged uterus and palpated a small left adnexal mass. Laboratory exams confirmed malaria and pregnancy. Her EVD test was negative. Due to the absence of an ultrasound machine in the ETU, pelvic ultrasound was impossible. Supportive treatment was initiated. Nineteen hours after admission, the patient developed an acute surgical abdomen, with progressive left-sided pelvic pain, abdominal distension, and diffuse tenderness. A ruptured ectopic pregnancy was suspected and confirmed by diagnostic abdominal puncture, which revealed hemoperitoneum. She developed hemorrhagic shock. However, the ETU lacked an operating room, surgical equipment, and anesthetic drugs. Transfer to another ETU was not feasible due to the absence of a surgical-capable facility, while transfer to a community facility required at least a second negative EVD test 48 h after the first. We used an unused building that was part of the neighboring health center to perform an explorative laparotomy, which revealed an EP in the left tube with ampullary dislocation and active bleeding. We performed a left salpingectomy and homeostatic sutures. The real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) tests for EVD performed at 48 and 72 h were negative. She had a normal postoperative course and was discharged from ETU to the community on the 14th day after surgery. Conclusion Ruptured ectopic pregnancy (REP) remains a life-threatening obstetric emergency that may occur in ETUs, where resources and treatment conditions are limited. To protect maternal health, ETUs should ensure not only proper care for Ebola infection but also adequate capacity for the timely diagnosis and management of obstetric complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the challenges of managing ruptured ectopic pregnancy in an Ebola treatment unit (ETU) where resources are limited and the focus is primarily on Ebola virus disease (EVD). The background emphasized that ectopic pregnancy is an obstetric emergency requiring early diagnosis for maternal survival.
Design and population
This case report describes a single pregnant woman in her 30s admitted to an ETU in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) with suspected Ebola virus disease. She had a history of recovery from an Ebola infection 13 months prior and presented with 8 weeks of amenorrhea and pelvic pain.
Core methods
The patient was managed using an unused building at the neighboring health center for an explorative laparotomy, which confirmed a ruptured ectopic pregnancy. Real-time polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) tests for EVD were conducted at 48 and 72 hours post-admission and were negative.
Main results
The patient developed hemorrhagic shock and was diagnosed with a ruptured ectopic pregnancy in the left fallopian tube. The RT-PCR tests for EVD at 48 and 72 hours were negative, confirming she was not infected with the Ebola virus at the time of presentation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a case of ruptured ectopic pregnancy occurring in an ETU, highlighting the lack of resources for managing obstetric emergencies. This case is novel as it is the first reported instance of such a complication in an ETU setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for ETUs to have adequate capacity for managing not only Ebola virus disease but also other obstetric complications that may arise in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable. The evidence is moderate as it is based on clinical observations and diagnostic procedures within an ETU, with no statistical analysis or broader population data.

当信仰与生物医学相冲突

When faith and biomedicine collide
作者： Nancy S. Jecker, Rose Mary Amenga-Etego, Caesar A. Atuire
摘要中文翻译本章探讨在传染病暴发期间,基于信仰与生物医学的健康、疾病与死亡观之间的冲突。通过基于病例与情境的分析方法,我们展示了生命伦理学在化解紧张局势、寻找共同立场方面的关键作用。第5.1节介绍塞拉利昂在埃博拉病毒病 暴发期间传统葬礼习俗的一个案例。第5.2节探讨乌干达在人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)/获得性免疫缺陷综合征(AIDS)流行期间,宗教驱动的仅禁欲倡议替代循证方法的案例。第5.3节分析加纳东北地区某巫术社区在2019新型冠状病毒(COVID-19)大流行期间疫苗拒绝的案例。在整个案例分析中,本章构建并阐释了文明、内部变革、认识论正义与平等尊重等伦理原则。我们展示这些原则如何引导公共卫生工作者在伦理层面跨越差异,有效保障健康与生命。第5.4节(结论)呼吁在生物医学与信仰驱动的健康与疾病观交汇处加强生命伦理学参与。

问题与背景
本章探讨在传染病暴发期间,基于信仰与生物医学的健康、疾病与死亡观之间的冲突。

通过基于病例与情境的分析方法,我们展示了生命伦理学在化解紧张局势、寻找共同立场方面的关键作用。
核心方法
第5.1节介绍塞拉利昂在埃博拉病毒病 暴发期间传统葬礼习俗的一个案例。
主要结果
第5.2节探讨乌干达在人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)/获得性免疫缺陷综合征(AIDS)流行期间,宗教驱动的仅禁欲倡议替代循证方法的案例。
解释与创新
第5.3节分析加纳东北地区某巫术社区在2019新型冠状病毒(COVID-19)大流行期间疫苗拒绝的案例。
科研与公卫意义
在整个案例分析中,本章构建并阐释了文明、内部变革、认识论正义与平等尊重等伦理原则。
局限与证据强度
我们展示这些原则如何引导公共卫生工作者在伦理层面跨越差异,有效保障健康与生命。

When faith and biomedicine collide

Authors: Nancy S. Jecker, Rose Mary Amenga-Etego, Caesar A. Atuire
Original abstract (English)
This chapter examines conflicts between faith- and biomedicine-based approaches to health, illness, and death during infectious disease outbreaks. Using a case-based and context-informed approach, we show the crucial contribution bioethics can make to resolving tensions and finding common ground. Section 5.1 introduces a case involving traditional burial practices in Sierra Leone during the Ebola virus disease outbreak. Section 5.2 considers a case in Uganda during the human immunodeficiency virus (HIV)/acquired immune deficiency syndrome (AIDS) epidemic involving religiously driven efforts to substitute abstinence-only initiatives for evidence-based methods. Section 5.3 explores a case of vaccine refusal at a witch community in the North East region of Ghana during the novel coronavirus 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic. Throughout the case analyses, the chapter develops and illustrates ethical principles of civility, change from within, epistemic justice, and equal respect. We show how these principles orient public health workers to ethically bridge differences and effectively safeguard health and life. The chapter concludes (in Section 5.4) with a bid for greater engagement of bioethics at the crossroads of biomedical and faith-based understandings of health and disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This chapter examines conflicts between faith- and biomedicine-based approaches to health, illness, and death during infectious disease outbreaks.
Design and population
Using a case-based and context-informed approach, we show the crucial contribution bioethics can make to resolving tensions and finding common ground.
Core methods
Section 5.1 introduces a case involving traditional burial practices in Sierra Leone during the Ebola virus disease outbreak.
Main results
Section 5.2 considers a case in Uganda during the human immunodeficiency virus (HIV)/acquired immune deficiency syndrome (AIDS) epidemic involving religiously driven efforts to substitute abstinence-only initiatives for evidence-based methods.
Interpretation and novelty
Section 5.3 explores a case of vaccine refusal at a witch community in the North East region of Ghana during the novel coronavirus 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
Throughout the case analyses, the chapter develops and illustrates ethical principles of civility, change from within, epistemic justice, and equal respect.
Limitations and evidence strength
We show how these principles orient public health workers to ethically bridge differences and effectively safeguard health and life.

超越埃博拉:武装冲突与人道主义资金削减威胁刚果民主共和国东部地区疟疾、结核与艾滋病的防控

Beyond Ebola: armed conflict and humanitarian funding cuts threaten control of malaria, TB and HIV in eastern DRC
作者： Roland Muhindo Muyisa, Vivuya Kavugho Loando, Louange Kavira Kavulivwa, Diane Katsuva Ngowire, Jean Paul Paluku Mwalitsa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨武装冲突与人道主义资金削减对刚果民主共和国东部地区疟疾、结核病与艾滋病等传染病防控的影响,并强调超越 埃博拉病毒 暴发 的公共卫生更广泛影响。
设计与对象
本研究为一项针对刚果民主共和国东部地区人道主义与健康危机的描述性分析,重点关注冲突与资金减少对埃博拉、疟疾、结核病与艾滋病疾病 监测、预防与 治疗 项目的影响。
核心方法
本研究采用定性与定量数据,来源于刚果民主共和国东部地区人道主义局势的报告与分析,包括流离失所人群统计、医疗基础设施损坏与服务中断等数据,以评估其对疾病防控项目的影响。
主要结果
人道主义资金削减已扰乱疫情 监测 与药品采购等关键职能,危及疟疾与结核病项目,并威胁增加发病率与 死亡率。抗疟药、结核病药物、抗逆转录病毒治疗药物、快速诊断试剂及个人防护装备的缺货可迅速将可控疾病转化为突发公共卫生事件。
解释与创新
作者将研究发现解读为当前埃博拉 暴发 是由冲突与人道主义紧缩驱动的更大协同流行的证据,强调需采用综合应对措施同时处理多重健康威胁。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需采用综合策略加强卫生系统韧性,并确保对疾病防控项目的持续投资,特别是在多种传染病相互作用的冲突影响地区。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于对报告数据的依赖,未纳入直接实证测量或统计分析。证据强度为中等,基于观察性数据与现有报告的推论。

Beyond Ebola: armed conflict and humanitarian funding cuts threaten control of malaria, TB and HIV in eastern DRC

Authors: Roland Muhindo Muyisa, Vivuya Kavugho Loando, Louange Kavira Kavulivwa, Diane Katsuva Ngowire, Jean Paul Paluku Mwalitsa
Original abstract (English)
Subjects The ongoing Ebola virus outbreak in the eastern part of the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), declared a Public Health Emergency by the World Health Organization (WHO) on 17 May 2026, is set against a broader humanitarian crisis1. Armed conflict, mass displacement and reduced humanitarian funding are undermining disease surveillance, prevention and treatment not only for Ebola but also for malaria, tuberculosis (TB) and HIV, creating a syndemic that threatens years of progress in disease control. With more than 7 million internally displaced individuals and deteriorating health infrastructure, the region’s capacity to respond to outbreaks is critically weakened2,3. Disruptions to health services and access to safe water fuel the spread of epidemic-prone diseases while limiting access to diagnosis and treatment. Recent cuts in humanitarian assistance have exacerbated the situation by disrupting essential functions such as epidemic surveillance and medicine procurement4,5, jeopardizing programs for malaria and TB and threatening increased morbidity and mortality. Therefore, the current Ebola outbreak should be viewed as part of a larger syndemic driven by conflict and humanitarian retrenchment. Effective epidemic preparedness in eastern DRC requires integrated strategies to strengthen health-system resilience while maintaining critical programs for malaria, TB and HIV. Without sustained investment, the vulnerabilities of one of the world’s most fragile health systems will remain exposed and exacerbated. Conflict and humanitarian funding cuts threaten far more than the Ebola response; they increasingly disrupt essential malaria, TB and HIV services in eastern DRC. Insecurity damages health facilities, weakens supply chains, and interrupts diagnosis, treatme...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the impact of armed conflict and humanitarian funding cuts on the control of infectious diseases such as malaria, tuberculosis (TB) and HIV in eastern DRC, highlighting the broader implications for public health beyond the Ebola virus outbreak.
Design and population
The study is a descriptive analysis of the ongoing humanitarian and health crisis in eastern DRC, focusing on the effects of conflict and funding reductions on disease surveillance, prevention and treatment programs for Ebola, malaria, TB and HIV.
Core methods
The study uses qualitative and quantitative data from reports and analyses of the humanitarian situation in eastern DRC, including statistics on displaced populations, health infrastructure damage and service disruptions, to evaluate the impact on disease control programs.
Main results
Humanitarian funding cuts have disrupted essential functions such as epidemic surveillance and medicine procurement, jeopardizing programs for malaria and TB and threatening increased morbidity and mortality. Stock-outs of antimalarial medicines, TB drugs, antiretroviral therapy, rapid diagnostic tests and personal protective equipment can rapidly transform manageable diseases into public health emergencies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that the current Ebola outbreak is part of a larger syndemic driven by conflict and humanitarian retrenchment, emphasizing the need for integrated responses to address multiple health threats simultaneously.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated strategies to strengthen health-system resilience and ensure sustained investment in disease control programs, particularly in conflict-affected regions where multiple infectious diseases interact.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported data and does not include direct empirical measurements or statistical analyses. The evidence is moderate, based on observational data and implications from existing reports.

东非丝状病毒暴发的叙事综述:加强区域疾病监测的经验教训

A narrative review of filovirus outbreaks in East Africa: lessons for strengthening regional disease surveillance
作者： Adewunmi Akingbola, Abiodun Adegbesan, Kolade Adegoke, Favour Peters, Joshua Alabi, Olajumoke Adewole, Petra Mariaria, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher

A narrative review of filovirus outbreaks in East Africa: lessons for strengthening regional disease surveillance

Authors: Adewunmi Akingbola, Abiodun Adegbesan, Kolade Adegoke, Favour Peters, Joshua Alabi, Olajumoke Adewole, Petra Mariaria, Adegbesan Abiodun Christopher
Original abstract (English)
The recent outbreaks of Marburg virus disease (MVD) in Tanzania and Sudan virus disease (SVD) in Uganda reveal the growing threat of filoviruses in East Africa. These highly lethal viruses, with case fatality rates exceeding 50%, continue to challenge regional health systems due to delayed detection, weak surveillance networks, and limited cross-border coordination. The outbreak in Tanzania’s Kagera region, a major transit hub, highlights the risk of international spread, while Uganda’s struggle with Sudan virus further emphasizes the urgent need for regional infectious disease surveillance. This review paper explores the epidemiological trends, challenges in outbreak response, and the role of regional cooperation in mitigating future filovirus threats. It summarizes key epidemiological features, transmission dynamics, and response timelines associated with both outbreaks to provide data-driven context to the current regional public health threat. By analyzing past and ongoing outbreaks, we propose strategies to strengthen disease surveillance, enhance cross-border coordination, and improve rapid response mechanisms. A unified regional approach is critical to preventing filovirus epidemics from escalating into global public health crises.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The recent outbreaks of Marburg virus disease (MVD) in Tanzania and Sudan virus disease (SVD) in Uganda reveal the growing threat of filoviruses in East Africa.
Evidence base and method
This review paper explores the epidemiological trends, challenges in outbreak response, and the role of regional cooperation in mitigating future filovirus threats.
Consensus and conclusions
It summarizes key epidemiological features, transmission dynamics, and response timelines associated with both outbreaks to provide data-driven context to the current regional public health threat.
Controversies and gaps
These highly lethal viruses, with case fatality rates exceeding 50%, continue to challenge regional health systems due to delayed detection, weak surveillance networks, and limited cross-border coordination.
Research and practice implications
By analyzing past and ongoing outbreaks, we propose strategies to strengthen disease surveillance, enhance cross-border coordination, and improve rapid response mechanisms.

刚果民主共和国与乌干达境内最大规模的邦迪布焦病毒疾病暴发

Beyond Ebola Virus in the Largest Documented Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak
作者： Damian Stodulski, Natalia Lopez-Escobar, Beatriz Cabanillas
摘要中文翻译刚果民主共和国与乌干达境内正在进行的邦迪布焦病毒病疫情已成为迄今记录的由该正型埃博拉病毒引起的最大规模疫情,且已跻身历史上第二大埃博拉病毒病疫情之列。本综述强调了其重大公共卫生意义,包括跨境传播、显著的发病率负担、高临床严重程度、医护人员感染以及缺乏针对邦迪布焦病毒病的任何获批抗病毒药物或特异性治疗方案。我们讨论了此次异常事件的流行病学与病毒学意义、当前诊断与治疗准备工作在超出埃博拉病毒病范围后的局限性,以及迫切需要更广泛的丝状病毒聚焦策略,包括改进诊断、扩大疫苗接种能力、加速候选抗病毒药物研发、开发非埃博拉丝状病毒疫苗平台,以及构建韧性的当地卫生系统。此次邦迪布焦病毒病疫情表明,主要针对埃博拉病毒病的准备工作已为其他人类致病性正型埃博拉病毒留下了大量缺口。

范围与问题
刚果民主共和国与乌干达境内的邦迪布焦病毒病疫情——迄今记录的最大规模邦迪布焦病毒病疫情,以及历史上第二大埃博拉病毒病疫情——及迫切需要更广泛的丝状病毒聚焦准备策略(超出埃博拉病毒病范围)。

共识与结论
此次邦迪布焦病毒病疫情因跨境传播(刚果民主共和国与乌干达间)、显著的发病率负担、高临床严重程度、医护人员感染以及缺乏针对邦迪布焦病毒病的任何获批抗病毒药物或特异性治疗方案,具有重大公共卫生意义。本综述将其定性为一次异常的流行病学与病毒学事件。

Beyond Ebola Virus in the Largest Documented Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak

Authors: Damian Stodulski, Natalia Lopez-Escobar, Beatriz Cabanillas
Original abstract (English)
The ongoing Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda has become the largest documented outbreak caused by this orthoebolavirus and already ranks as the second-largest Ebola disease outbreak recorded in history. This review highlights its major public health relevance, given its cross-border spread, substantial mortality burden, high clinical severity, infections among healthcare workers, and the lack of any approved vaccine or specific treatment for Bundibugyo virus disease. We discuss the epidemiological and virological significance of this exceptional event, the limitations of current diagnostic and therapeutic preparedness beyond Ebola virus, and the urgent need for broader filovirus-focused strategies, including improved diagnostics, genomic surveillance, candidate vaccines, antiviral platforms, and resilient local health systems. The outbreak illustrates how preparedness efforts centered mainly on the Ebola virus have left substantial gaps for other human-pathogenic orthoebolaviruses.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, the largest documented Bundibugyo outbreak and second-largest Ebola disease outbreak recorded in history, and the urgent need for broader filovirus-focused preparedness strategies beyond Ebola virus.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report the review type, databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, selection method, included-study count, or meta-analytic process.
Consensus and conclusions
The outbreak demonstrates substantial public health relevance due to cross-border spread between the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, high mortality burden, elevated clinical severity, infections among healthcare workers, and the absence of any approved vaccine or specific treatment for Bundibugyo virus disease. The review characterizes this as an exceptional epidemiological and virological event.
Controversies and gaps
Preparedness efforts centered mainly on Ebola virus have left substantial gaps for other human-pathogenic orthoebolaviruses. Current diagnostic and therapeutic preparedness is limited beyond Ebola virus, and broader filovirus-focused strategies remain underdeveloped.
Research and practice implications
Urgent priorities include improved diagnostics specific to Bundibugyo and related orthoebolaviruses, expanded genomic surveillance capacity, accelerated development of candidate vaccines and antiviral platforms for non-Ebola filoviruses, and strengthening of resilient local health systems in endemic regions to prevent cross-border transmission and healthcare worker infections.

英国临床医学生对A类生物恐怖剂综合征识别能力的研究:一项先导研究

Recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes by final-year medical students in the UK: a pilot study
作者： Richard Armitage, C. Hammer, Armitage, R. C., Hammer, C. C.
摘要中文翻译背景:早期识别与A类生物恐怖剂故意释放相符的临床表现,对于快速隔离、公共卫生通报及疫情控制至关重要。英国培训中的临床医生识别此类综合征的能力尚未得到研究。本先导研究评估了英国临床医学生识别此类综合征的能力。方法:本研究采用横断面在线调查,对英国临床医学生展示与A类生物恐怖剂相关综合征(生物恐怖剂 vignettes,BT vignettes)及临床上重叠的非生物恐怖剂综合征(非生物恐怖剂 vignettes,NBT vignettes)的单一最佳答案临床病例 vignettes。以正确识别vignettes的比例总结表现,主要分析比较参与者在BT与NBT中的表现。结果:共有25名参与者完成调查。参与者在BT vignettes上的表现(均值M=0.55)劣于NBT vignettes(均值M=0.81),参与者内差异为-0.26(95% CI [-0.35,-0.18];t(24) = -6.33,p < 0.001;Cohen's dz = -1.27)。大多数参与者能识别肉毒中毒(96.0%)和埃博拉病毒病(88.0%),但识别炭疽(40.0%)、肺鼠疫(28.0%)和天花(24.0%)的比例不足一半。结论:本先导研究首次为英国临床医学生在识别A类生物恐怖剂综合征方面存在显著诊断缺陷提供了证据。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究审查英国临床医学生识别A类生物恐怖剂相关综合征(包括埃博拉病毒病)的能力,作为早期临床识别以实施隔离及公共卫生应对的一部分。
证据基础与方法
本研究报告了一项先导横断面在线调查,通过单一最佳答案临床病例vignettes评估英国临床医学生对A类生物恐怖剂综合征(BT vignettes)与临床上重叠的非生物恐怖剂综合征(NBT vignettes)的识别能力。
共识与结论
本研究得出结论:英国临床医学生在BT vignettes上的表现(均值0.55)显著劣于NBT vignettes(均值0.81),参与者内差异为-0.26(95% CI [-0.35,-0.18];p < 0.001;Cohen's dz = -1.27)。
争议与缺口
本研究强调了英国临床医学生在识别A类生物恐怖剂综合征方面存在显著诊断缺陷,特别是炭疽(40.0%)、肺鼠疫(28.0%)和天花(24.0%)的识别率较低,尽管肉毒中毒(96.0%)和埃博拉病毒病(88.0%)的识别率较高。
科研与实践启示
本研究表明,英国临床医学生亟需针对性的教育干预,以提高对A类生物恐怖剂综合征的识别能力,特别是对炭疽、肺鼠疫和天花等识别率较低的病原体。

Recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes by final-year medical students in the UK: a pilot study

Authors: Richard Armitage, C. Hammer, Armitage, R. C., Hammer, C. C.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Early recognition of presentations consistent with the deliberate release of a Category A bioterrorism agent is essential for rapid isolation, public health notification, and containment. The ability of UK clinicians-in-training to recognise these syndromes is unstudied. This pilot assessed final-year UK medical students' ability to recognise these syndromes. Methods A pilot cross-sectional online survey of final-year UK medical students used single-best-answer clinical vignettes depicting syndromes associated with Category A bioterrorism agents (BT vignettes) and clinically overlapping non-bioterrorism syndromes (NBT vignettes). Performance was summarised as the proportion of vignettes correctly identified, with primary analysis comparing within-participant BT and NBT performance. Results Twenty-five participants completed the survey. Participants performed worse on BT vignettes (M = 0.55) than on NBT vignettes (M = 0.81), with a within-participant difference of -0.26 (95% CI [-0.35, -0.18]; t(24) = -6.33, p < 0.001; Cohen's dz = -1.27). Botulism (96.0%) and Ebola virus disease (88.0%) were recognised by most participants, while anthrax (40.0%), pneumonic plague (28.0%), and smallpox (24.0%) were recognised by fewer than half. Conclusion This pilot provides the first UK evidence of a substantial diagnostic deficit in final-year medical students' recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the ability of final-year UK medical students to recognise syndromes associated with Category A bioterrorism agents, including Ebola virus disease, as part of early clinical recognition for isolation and public health response.
Evidence base and method
The review reports a pilot cross-sectional online survey using single-best-answer clinical vignettes to assess recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes (BT vignettes) versus clinically overlapping non-bioterrorism syndromes (NBT vignettes) among final-year UK medical students.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that final-year UK medical students performed significantly worse on BT vignettes (mean 0.55) than on NBT vignettes (mean 0.81), with a within-participant difference of -0.26 (95% CI [-0.35, -0.18]; p < 0.001; Cohen's dz = -1.27).
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights a substantial diagnostic deficit in recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes, particularly for anthrax (40.0%), pneumonic plague (28.0%), and smallpox (24.0%), despite high recognition of botulism (96.0%) and Ebola virus disease (88.0%).
Research and practice implications
The review implies a need for targeted educational interventions to improve recognition of Category A bioterrorism agent syndromes among UK medical students, particularly for less-recognised agents such as anthrax, pneumonic plague, and smallpox.
